# Agents · R · part 32

1,152 entries, reviewer to reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **reviewer**: Revue de code en lecture seule — conventions, correction, architecture. Premier nœud de la revue en fan-out de review-story. N'écrit pas de code, ne corrige rien, ne commente pas de PR. Trigger — EN: review, code review, audit, find bugs, code quality, conventions. Trigger — FR: revue, revue de code, audit, qualité du code, conventions. &lt;example&gt; user: 'Revois le diff de la story avant la PR' assistant: 'Using reviewer: conventions, correction et architecture sur le diff, en lecture seule.' &lt;/example&gt; ([jamyl/socle](https://github.com/jamyl/socle/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/reviewer.md) · ★1)

Part 1이 FAIL이어도 Part 2를 수행해 재구현 라운드에 품질 지적을 함께 전달한다. oh-my-gx:gx-tdd phase-review(전체 브랜치)와 phase-implement Step 2-V(태스크 범위 모드) 전용 — 구 spec-reviewer/quality-reviewer 2석을 대체한다. &lt;example&gt; Context: phase-review Step 2 진입 user: (오케스트레이터) diff가 PRD의 AC를 충족하는지, 코드 품질이 적절한지 한 번에 리뷰해줘 assistant: reviewer가 Part 1에서 AC 매트릭스(AC-1 ✅, AC-2 ❌)와 spec_verdict FAIL을 확정한 뒤, Part 2에서 Critical 0·Important 2건([동작불변])과 quality_verdict를 보고 — AC-2 관련 지적은 "재구현 대상"으로 표기 &lt;/example&gt; &lt;… ([bs-koo/oh-my-gx](https://github.com/bs-koo/oh-my-gx/blob/HEAD/agents/reviewer.md))
